commit a41bd8e480b778689f78bd4ff8611240130efd84
Author: developer <dev@medianest.info>
Date:   Fri Sep 11 21:54:22 2026 +0530

    feat: Separate Media Studio and Video Editor into standalone Add-On App (:studio)
    
    - Stripped native C++ encoders (x264, x265, aom, vpx) from main app (:app) to reduce release APK size to ~12 MB.
    - Configured medianest_lib C++ target in :app for decoder-only corrupted media playback fallback.
    - Extracted Media Studio & Video Editor into standalone module (:studio / com.medianest.studio).
    - Implemented inter-app communication via StudioIntegrationManager with ACTION_EDIT_MEDIA and ACTION_EDIT_VIDEO intent hand-offs.
    - Added queries block in :app AndroidManifest.xml for Android 11+ API 30+ package visibility.
    - Added StudioInstallDialog for seamless add-on installation prompt when :studio is not installed.

commit d346ec46ee3c8afc6bf701bbbb96c73f493ce1eb
Author: developer <dev@medianest.info>
Date:   Fri Sep 11 11:29:49 2026 +0530

    fix(player): remove playbackPositionState from VideoPlayerScreen root level
    
    The root cause of video freezing after pause/resume was collecting
    playbackPositionState at the top of VideoPlayerScreen (1620 lines).
    This caused the entire screen to recompose every 200ms during playback,
    which repeatedly invalidated the graphicsLayer RenderNode wrapping the
    SurfaceView.
    
    SurfaceView renders in a separate SurfaceFlinger overlay plane. Every
    graphicsLayer RenderNode invalidation forces HWUI and SurfaceFlinger to
    re-negotiate that overlay. During active playback the video decoder
    continuously resubmits frames so the surface self-heals. But during
    pause the decoder is idle — if the overlay is dropped mid-pause the
    surface is never recovered. Pressing play then resumed audio (AudioTrack
    is unaffected) but video had no valid surface: audio-only playback.
    
    Fix: removed playbackPositionState collection from VideoPlayerScreen.
    playerState already carries currentPositionMs/durationMs/isPlaying and
    updates every ~200ms via the position-change threshold (>= 100ms).
    VideoPlayerBottomBar and CenterTransportControls now use playerState
    directly via their existing default params — seek bar update rate is
    unchanged, and VideoPlayerScreen no longer recomposes from a second flow.

 .../java/com/medianest/ui/videoplayer/VideoPlayerScreen.kt     | 10 +---------
 1 file changed, 1 insertion(+), 9 deletions(-)

commit 1190011868a5f1c49bcd1d77630bf4dac1ee9329
Author: developer <dev@medianest.info>
Date:   Fri Sep 11 11:22:37 2026 +0530

    fix(player): fix video/audio freeze after pause-resume due to audio focus bugs
    
    Two compounding bugs caused playback to freeze when pressing play after pause:
    
    1. abandonAudioFocus() was called on every pause(). This triggered an async
       AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS callback on the registered listener which called
       activeEngine?.pause() again — right as play() was trying to resume.
       Result: engine paused itself immediately after the user pressed play.
       Fix: removed abandonAudioFocus() from pause(). Focus is now only
       abandoned on stop() and release() where it belongs.
    
    2. requestAudioFocus() with setAcceptsDelayedFocusGain(true) can return
       AUDIOFOCUS_REQUEST_DELAYED (2) instead of AUDIOFOCUS_REQUEST_GRANTED (1).
       The old check (== GRANTED) returned false, so play() was silently skipped.
       Audio sometimes drained from ExoPlayer internal buffers giving the illusion
       of audio-only playback. Fix: handle DELAYED by setting playOnFocusGain=true
       so the AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN callback resumes playback when focus arrives.
    
    3. AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS in the listener now calls pause() (full function) instead
       of activeEngine?.pause() directly, keeping playerState.isPlaying in sync.

 .../java/com/medianest/player/ExoPlayerManager.kt  | 24 ++++++++++++++++++----
 1 file changed, 20 insertions(+), 4 deletions(-)

commit 14afd498bc3f5980a2a3d2ed09acda0e1ca9c215
Author: developer <dev@medianest.info>

commit 67884f192b1c992ef2d79df6aa5ba250fcc17066
Author: developer <dev@medianest.info>
Date:   Thu Aug 13 14:43:13 2026 +0530

    Integrated Native Oboe Audio Path and FFmpeg DSP Engine
    
    - Audio Architecture: Migrated native audio rendering to Google Oboe (AAudio/OpenSL ES) for direct hardware access and ultra-low latency.
    - DSP Engine: Implemented FFmpeg libavfilter graph in C++ supporting real-time High Bass Boost, 5-band Equalizer, and Super Volume Boost (up to 300%).
    - Universal Format Support: Extended native engine to handle Hi-Fi audio formats (FLAC, ALAC, WAV) and standard audio (MP3, AAC) with native DSP enhancements.
    - A/V Sync: Hardened master clock synchronization slaved to the Oboe hardware clock with sub-millisecond drift correction.
    - UI: Added 'Native Audio DSP' control panels to both Music and Video players.
    - Documentation: Updated README.md with a detailed Mermaid architecture diagram and engine selection matrix.
    - Dependencies: Integrated Oboe via Prefab and added missing libavfilter headers.

    Implement fault-tolerant media player with Media3 and FFmpeg fallback
    
    - Architecture: Introduced PlaybackEngine interface and implemented Media3PlaybackEngine and FFmpegPlaybackEngine.
    - Engine Routing: Added MediaCapabilityInspector to route legacy/corrupt files (AVI, AC-3) to FFmpeg.

    - Stability: Fixed several initialization NPEs and lifecycle issues in ExoPlayerManager.
    - Refactoring: Cleaned up series name parsing in VideoFilterUtils.kt.
